# Graph Report - NVLabsCompany  (2026-08-30)

## Corpus Check
- 1554 files · ~1,421,881 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 24853 nodes · 40689 edges · 1251 communities (1085 shown, 166 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2207 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e8a19409`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
